    a = sub.add_parser("inspect", help="inspect a model artifact")
    a.add_argument("model"); a.add_argument("--json", action="store_true")
    a.set_defaults(fn=cmd_inspect)

    a = sub.add_parser("qualify", help="measure this machine")
    a.add_argument("model", nargs="?", default="")
    a.set_defaults(fn=cmd_qualify)

    a = sub.add_parser("requalify", help="force re-measurement")
    a.add_argument("model", nargs="?", default="")
    a.set_defaults(fn=cmd_qualify)

    a = sub.add_parser("plan", help="build an execution plan")
    a.add_argument("model")
    a.add_argument("--context", type=int, default=8192)
    a.add_argument("--concurrency", type=int, default=1)
    a.add_argument("--workload", default="auto",
                   choices=["auto", "general", "coding", "math", "json", "longctx"])
    a.add_argument("--json", action="store_true")
    a.set_defaults(fn=cmd_plan)

    a = sub.add_parser("selftest", help="prove exact expert execution")
    a.add_argument("model", nargs="?", default="")
    a.set_defaults(fn=cmd_selftest)

    a = sub.add_parser("bench", help="MEASURED per-block throughput")
    a.add_argument("model", nargs="?", default="")
    a.add_argument("--trace", default="")
    a.add_argument("--block", type=int, default=0)
    a.add_argument("--tokens", type=int, default=1000)
    a.add_argument("--slots", default="16,64,256,512")
    a.add_argument("--bank-mode", default="mmap", choices=["mmap", "ram"])
    a.add_argument("--json", default="")
    a.add_argument("--allow-low-clock", action="store_true",
                   help="exit 0 even if the steady SM clock was below 2400 MHz")
    a.set_defaults(fn=cmd_bench)

    a = sub.add_parser("benchmark", help="measure the full-model reference path")
    a.add_argument("model")
    a.add_argument("--prompt", default="Reply with exactly: SABAH_OK")
    a.add_argument("--tokens", type=int, default=16)
    a.add_argument("--context", type=int, default=4096)
    a.add_argument("--threads", type=int, default=0)
    a.add_argument("--llama-cli", default="")
    a.add_argument("--json", default="")
    a.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
    a.set_defaults(fn=cmd_benchmark)

    a = sub.add_parser("serve", help="serve a local OpenAI-compatible API")
    a.add_argument("model")
    a.add_argument("--host", default="127.0.0.1")
    a.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=8080)
    a.add_argument("--backend-port", type=int, default=18080)
    a.add_argument("--context", type=int, default=4096)
    a.add_argument("--n-gpu-layers", type=int, default=99)
    a.add_argument("--backend", choices=["sabah", "reference"], default=None)
    a.add_argument("--hot-bytes", type=int, default=1 << 30,
                   help="VRAM budget for Sabah's expert hot tier")
    a.add_argument("--parallel", type=int, default=4, help="server slots")
    a.add_argument("--validate", action="store_true",
                   help="self-validating Sabah backend (float64 self-check, byte verification)")
    a.add_argument("--llama-server", default="")
    a.add_argument("--allow-reference", action="store_true",
                   help="required with --backend reference: stock llama.cpp expert execution")
    a.add_argument("--quiet", action="store_true")
    a.set_defaults(fn=cmd_serve)

    a = sub.add_parser("calibrate", help="re-measure the planner hit curve")
    a.add_argument("model", nargs="?", default="")
    a.add_argument("--trace", default="")
    a.add_argument("--json", default="")
    a.set_defaults(fn=cmd_calibrate)

    def _stream_args(a, default_tokens):
        a.add_argument("model")
        a.add_argument("--prompt", default="Hello")
        a.add_argument("--tokens", type=int, default=default_tokens)
        a.add_argument("--context", type=int, default=512)
        a.add_argument("--threads", type=int, default=8)
        a.add_argument("--hot-bytes", default="9.8e9",
                       help="VRAM budget for the expert tier (9.8e9, 9.8G, or bytes)")
        a.add_argument("--diag", default="",
                       help="path to llama-sabah-diag.exe (or SABAH_DIAG_EXE)")
        a.add_argument("--rt", default="",
                       help="path to sabah_rt.dll (or SABAH_RT_LIB)")

    a = sub.add_parser("run", help="one command: the shipped streaming decode")
    _stream_args(a, 256)
    a.add_argument("--json", default="")
    a.add_argument("--set", action="append", default=[], metavar="KEY=VALUE",
                   help="extra runtime environment, repeatable")
    a.set_defaults(fn=cmd_run)

    a = sub.add_parser("matrix", help="regenerate results/matrix.json")
    _stream_args(a, 32)
    a.add_argument("--tokens-verify", type=int, default=8)
    a.add_argument("--out", default="")
    a.set_defaults(fn=cmd_matrix)

    a = sub.add_parser("gate", help="correctness switches as pass/fail")
    _stream_args(a, 8)
    a.add_argument("--verify-limit", type=int, default=2000)
    a.set_defaults(fn=cmd_gate)

    a = sub.add_parser("doctor", help="environment check")
    a.set_defaults(fn=cmd_doctor)

    a = sub.add_parser("status", help="show saved profiles")
    a.set_defaults(fn=cmd_status)

    args = ap.parse_args(argv)
    if not getattr(args, "fn", None):
        ap.print_help()
        return 1
    return args.fn(args)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


===== sabah/tools/clocks.py (pass 1) =====

"""GPU clock hygiene for benchmark records.

A measurement is only reproducible if the card ran at boost. This module gives
every benchmark the same three things:

* ``ClockSampler`` - a 0.5 s-resolution nvidia-smi stream covering SM clock,
  memory clock, P-state, power, temperature, utilisation, display state and the
  throttle-reason bitmask;
* ``ClockSampler.record()`` - whole-run plus steady-window statistics, and a
  ``valid`` flag that is false when the steady-window median SM clock is below
  ``MIN_STEADY_SM_MHZ`` (2400 MHz);
* ``pin()`` / ``unpin()`` - the reversible ``nvidia-smi -lgc``/``-lmc``
  pinning that makes a run reproducible, and ``machine_state()`` - the
  machine-level settings that decide whether the card is allowed to downclock
  (Windows power plan, HAGS, NVIDIA power-management registry values, display
  state).

The driver is never modified. Pinning needs an elevated shell; ``pin`` reports
the nvidia-smi message when it is refused instead of raising.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import statistics
import subprocess
import threading
import time

MIN_STEADY_SM_MHZ = 2400.0
MIN_STEADY_MEM_MHZ = 10000.0

QUERY = ("clocks.sm,clocks.mem,pstate,power.draw,power.limit,temperature.gpu,"
         "utilization.gpu,fan.speed,display_active,display_attached,"
         "clocks_throttle_reasons.active")

_NUM = {
    "sm_mhz": "clocks.sm",
    "mem_mhz": "clocks.mem",
    "power_w": "power.draw",
    "power_limit_w": "power.limit",
    "temp_c": "temperature.gpu",
    "util_pct": "utilization.gpu",
    "fan_pct": "fan.speed",
}


def _num(text):
    try:
        v = float(text)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None
    return v


def parse_line(line):
    """One nvidia-smi csv row (``noheader,nounits``) to a dict."""
    parts = [p.strip() for p in line.split(",")]
    if len(parts) < 11:
        return None
    rec = {
        "sm_mhz": _num(parts[0]),
        "mem_mhz": _num(parts[1]),
        "pstate": parts[2] or None,
        "power_w": _num(parts[3]),
        "power_limit_w": _num(parts[4]),
        "temp_c": _num(parts[5]),
        "util_pct": _num(parts[6]),
        "fan_pct": _num(parts[7]),
        "display_active": parts[8] or None,
        "display_attached": parts[9] or None,
        "throttle": parts[10] or None,
    }
    if rec["sm_mhz"] is None:
        return None
    return rec


def sample_once():
    try:
        out = subprocess.run(
            ["nvidia-smi", f"--query-gpu={QUERY}",
             "--format=csv,noheader,nounits"],
            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15)
    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
        return None
    for line in out.stdout.splitlines():
        rec = parse_line(line)
        if rec is not None:
            return rec
    return None


def _agg(values):
    vals = [v for v in values if v is not None]
    if not vals:
        return None
    vals.sort()
    n = len(vals)
    med = vals[n // 2] if n % 2 else (vals[n // 2 - 1] + vals[n // 2]) / 2.0
    return {"n": n, "min": vals[0], "median": med, "max": vals[-1],
            "mean": round(sum(vals) / n, 3)}


def summarise(samples):
    """Statistics over ``samples`` (list of ``(t, dict)``)."""
    if not samples:
        return None
    rows = [s for _, s in samples]
    out = {}
    for key in ("sm_mhz", "mem_mhz", "power_w", "temp_c", "util_pct"):
        a = _agg([r.get(key) for r in rows])
        if a:
            out[key] = a
    pstates = {}
    for r in rows:
        if r.get("pstate"):
            pstates[r["pstate"]] = pstates.get(r["pstate"], 0) + 1
    out["pstates"] = dict(sorted(pstates.items()))
    out["dominant_pstate"] = (max(pstates, key=pstates.get) if pstates else None)
    out["throttle"] = sorted({r["throttle"] for r in rows if r.get("throttle")})
    out["display_active"] = sorted({r["display_active"] for r in rows
                                    if r.get("display_active")})
    out["samples"] = len(rows)
    out["span_s"] = round(samples[-1][0] - samples[0][0], 2)
    return out


def verdict(steady):
    """Validity of a steady-window summary."""
    if not steady or not steady.get("sm_mhz"):
        return {"valid": None, "reason": "no clock samples"}
    sm = steady["sm_mhz"]
    mem = (steady.get("mem_mhz") or {}).get("median")
    reasons = []
    if sm["median"] < MIN_STEADY_SM_MHZ:
        reasons.append(f"steady SM median {sm['median']:.0f} MHz < "
                       f"{MIN_STEADY_SM_MHZ:.0f} MHz (min {sm['min']:.0f}, "
                       f"max {sm['max']:.0f}, pstate {steady.get('dominant_pstate')})")
    if mem is not None and mem < MIN_STEADY_MEM_MHZ:
        reasons.append(f"steady memory median {mem:.0f} MHz < "
                       f"{MIN_STEADY_MEM_MHZ:.0f} MHz (the card dropped to a "
                       f"low P-state even if SM was pinned)")
    return {
        "valid": not reasons,
        "reason": "; ".join(reasons) if reasons else None,
        "min_steady_sm_mhz": MIN_STEADY_SM_MHZ,
        "min_steady_mem_mhz": MIN_STEADY_MEM_MHZ,
    }


class ClockSampler(threading.Thread):
    """Background nvidia-smi clock stream.

    ``interval`` is the sample period in seconds. Samples are kept with a
    monotonic timestamp so any window (whole run, steady decode, a specific
    episode) can be summarised afterwards.
    """

    def __init__(self, interval=0.5):
        super().__init__(daemon=True, name="clock-sampler")
        self.interval = float(interval)
        self.samples = []
        self._stop_evt = threading.Event()
        self._proc = None
        self.started_at = None

    def run(self):
        try:
            self._proc = subprocess.Popen(
                ["nvidia-smi", f"--query-gpu={QUERY}",
                 "--format=csv,noheader,nounits",
                 "-lms", str(max(50, int(self.interval * 1000)))],
                stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                text=True, bufsize=1)
        except OSError:
            return
        self.started_at = time.monotonic()
        for line in self._proc.stdout:
            if self._stop_evt.is_set():
                break
            rec = parse_line(line)
            if rec is not None:
                self.samples.append((time.monotonic(), rec))

    def stop(self):
        self._stop_evt.set()
        p = self._proc
        if p is not None:
            try:
                p.terminate()
                p.wait(timeout=5)
            except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
                pass
        self.join(timeout=5)
        return self

    def window(self, t0, t1=None):
        t1 = time.monotonic() if t1 is None else t1
        return [(t, s) for (t, s) in self.samples if t0 <= t <= t1]

    def record(self, decode_ms=None, end=None):
        """Whole-run and steady-window record.

        ``decode_ms`` is the summed per-token time the driver printed. The
        steady window is the tail of the run that the decodes occupy, plus a
        2 s margin for the sampler and the driver's final flush.
        """
        end = time.monotonic() if end is None else end
        whole = summarise(self.samples)
        rec = {"whole": whole, "query": QUERY,
               "interval_s": self.interval,
               "samples": [[round(t - (self.started_at or t), 3), s]
                           for t, s in self.samples]}
        steady = None
        if decode_ms:
            span = (float(decode_ms) + 2000.0) / 1000.0
            steady = summarise(self.window(end - span, end))
        if steady is None:
            # No decode information: the last third of the run is the best
            # available proxy for steady state.
            if self.samples:
                t0 = self.samples[0][0]
                steady = summarise(self.window(end - (end - t0) / 3.0, end))
        rec["steady"] = steady
        rec.update(verdict(steady))
        return rec


def pin(sm_min, sm_max, mem_min=None, mem_max=None):
    """Set application clock floors/ceilings with nvidia-smi (needs admin)."""
    out = []
    ok = True
    try:
        r = subprocess.run(["nvidia-smi", "-lgc", f"{int(sm_min)},{int(sm_max)}"],
                           capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
        ok = ok and r.returncode == 0
        out.append((r.stdout + r.stderr).strip())
        if mem_min is not None and mem_max is not None:
            r = subprocess.run(["nvidia-smi", "-lmc",
                                f"{int(mem_min)},{int(mem_max)}"],
                               capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
            ok = ok and r.returncode == 0
            out.append((r.stdout + r.stderr).strip())
    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as e:
        return {"ok": False, "output": str(e)}
    return {"ok": ok, "output": "\n".join(x for x in out if x)}


def unpin():
    """Restore default clock management (needs admin)."""
    out = []
    ok = True
    try:
        for flag in ("-rgc", "-rmc"):
            r = subprocess.run(["nvidia-smi", flag], capture_output=True,
                               text=True, timeout=30)
            ok = ok and r.returncode == 0
            out.append((r.stdout + r.stderr).strip())
    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as e:
        return {"ok": False, "output": str(e)}
    return {"ok": ok, "output": "\n".join(x for x in out if x)}


def _run(cmd, timeout=15):
    try:
        r = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout)
        return (r.stdout + r.stderr).strip()
    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
        return None


def _reg(path, value):
    out = _run(["reg", "query", path, "/v", value])
    if not out or "REG_" not in out:
        return None
    return out


def machine_state():
    """Settings that decide whether the card may downclock or lose boost."""
    plan = _run(["powercfg", "/getactivescheme"]) or ""
    plan = plan.replace("\r", "").replace("\n", " ").strip()
    graphics = r"HKLM\SYSTEM\CurrentControlSet\Control\GraphicsDrivers"
    cls = (r"HKLM\SYSTEM\CurrentControlSet\Control\Class"
           r"\{4d36e968-e325-11ce-bfc1-08002be10318}\0000")
    nv = {}
    for value in ("PowerMizerEnable", "PowerMizerLevel", "PowerMizerLevelAC",
                  "PerfLevelSrc", "DisableDynamicPstate", "PreferMaximumPerformance"):
        raw = _reg(cls, value)
        if raw:
            nv[value] = raw.split()[-1]
    out = {
        "power_plan": plan or None,
        "power_plan_guid": None,
        "hags_HwSchMode": None,
        "nvidia_registry": nv,
        "nvidia_smi": None,
    }
    if "GUID:" in plan:
        try:
            out["power_plan_guid"] = plan.split("GUID:")[1].split()[0]
        except IndexError:
            pass
    raw = _reg(graphics, "HwSchMode")
    out["hags_HwSchMode"] = raw.split()[-1] if raw else "absent (HAGS off)"
    try:
        r = subprocess.run(
            ["nvidia-smi", "--query-gpu=driver_version,power.limit,"
             "power.default_limit,power.max_limit,display_active,display_attached",
             "--format=csv,noheader"],
            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15)
        out["nvidia_smi"] = r.stdout.strip().splitlines()[0] if r.stdout.strip() else None
    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
        pass
    return out


def clock_series_csv(rec, path):
    """Write the raw samples of a ``record()`` dict as CSV."""
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write("t_s,sm_mhz,mem_mhz,pstate,power_w,temp_c,util_pct,throttle\n")
        for t, s in rec.get("samples", []):
            f.write(f"{t},{s.get('sm_mhz')},{s.get('mem_mhz')},{s.get('pstate')},"
                    f"{s.get('power_w')},{s.get('temp_c')},{s.get('util_pct')},"
                    f"{s.get('throttle')}\n")


def format_steady(rec):
    s = rec.get("steady") or {}
    sm = s.get("sm_mhz") or {}
    mem = s.get("mem_mhz") or {}
    pw = s.get("power_w") or {}
    flag = "VALID" if rec.get("valid") else "INVALID"
    if not sm:
        return f"clocks: {flag} (no samples)"
    return (f"clocks: {flag} steady SM {sm['min']:.0f}/{sm['median']:.0f}/"
            f"{sm['max']:.0f} MHz, mem {mem.get('median', 0):.0f} MHz, "
            f"{s.get('dominant_pstate')}, {pw.get('median', 0):.1f} W, "
            f"{s.get('throttle')}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print(json.dumps({"now": sample_once(), "machine": machine_state()}, indent=2))


===== sabah/tools/run_locality.py (pass 1) =====

"""
Sabah - locality test: same experts per block across all tokens.
Tests if Sabah can hit 10-15 tok/s when routing has expert locality.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import sys
import time
import argparse
import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "..", ".."))

from sabah.core.model_inspector import inspect_model
from sabah.runtime import rt
from sabah.runtime.expert_bank import ExpertBank
from sabah.runtime.hot_tier import HotTier
from sabah.runtime.executor import MoEExecutor
from sabah.runtime.residency import FixedPathResident


def main(argv=None):
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="sabah run-locality")
    ap.add_argument("--model", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--tokens", type=int, default=10)
    ap.add_argument("--warmup", type=int, default=3)
    ap.add_argument("--hot-bytes", type=int, default=4294967296)
    ap.add_argument("--bank-mode", default="ram", choices=["mmap", "ram"])
    ap.add_argument("--pin-fixed-path", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--json-out", default="")
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)

    if not rt.available() or rt.device_count() < 1:
        print("CUDA runtime unavailable."); return 2
    rt.check(rt.lib().sabah_rt_init(0), "rt_init")

    prof = inspect_model(args.model)
    if not prof.supported:
        print("unsupported model"); return 2

    fpr = None
    if args.pin_fixed_path:
        fpr = FixedPathResident(prof)
        fpr.load()
        print("fixed path: %.3f GB pinned in VRAM (%d tensors)"
              % (fpr.resident_bytes / 1e9, len(fpr._ptrs)))

    bank = ExpertBank(prof, mode=args.bank_mode,
                      blocks=list(range(prof.n_blocks)))

    tier = HotTier(bank, capacity_bytes=args.hot_bytes,
                   blocks=list(range(prof.n_blocks)),
                   prefetch_slots=0)
    print("hot tier : %.3f GB VRAM, %d slots/block"
          % (tier.capacity_bytes / 1e9, tier.slots_per_block))

    ex = MoEExecutor(bank, tier, max_used=prof.n_experts_used)

    # ---- LOCALITY: same 10 experts per block for all tokens -------------
    # Pick experts 0..9 for every block, every token.
    # This guarantees 100% hit rate after warmup.
    n_tokens = args.tokens + args.warmup
    traces = {}
    for b in range(prof.n_blocks):
        traces[b] = np.tile(np.arange(prof.n_experts_used, dtype=np.int32),
                            (n_tokens, 1))

    w = np.full(prof.n_experts_used, 1.0 / prof.n_experts_used, dtype=np.float32)
    x = (np.random.default_rng(7).standard_normal(prof.d_model)
         .astype(np.float32) * 0.05)

    # warmup
    print("warming up %d tokens..." % args.warmup)
    for t in range(args.warmup):
        for b in range(prof.n_blocks):
            ex.run_block(b, traces[b][t].tolist(), w)
    ex.sync()
    tier.tel.reset()

    # timed
    print("running %d tokens x %d blocks..." % (args.tokens, prof.n_blocks))
    rt.check(rt.lib().sabah_sync_all(), "pre-timer sync")
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    for t in range(args.tokens):
        for b in range(prof.n_blocks):
            ex.run_block(b, traces[b][args.warmup + t].tolist(), w)
    ex.sync()
    dt = time.perf_counter() - t0

    n_blocks_executed = args.tokens * prof.n_blocks
    ms_per_token = 1e3 * dt / args.tokens
    tok_s = args.tokens / dt
    blk_tok_s = n_blocks_executed / dt

    print("=" * 60)
    print("RESULT (LOCALITY): %.3f ms/token  ->  %.1f tok/s"
          % (ms_per_token, tok_s))
    print("tokens : %d" % args.tokens)
    print("blocks : %d x %d = %d" % (prof.n_blocks, args.tokens, n_blocks_executed))
    print("wall   : %.3f s" % dt)
    print("hit rate: %.4f" % tier.tel.hit_rate)
    print("bytes fetched: %.2f MB total" % (tier.tel.bytes_fetched / 1e6))
    print("=" * 60)

    if args.json_out:
        import json
        with open(args.json_out, "w") as f:
            json.dump(dict(
                model=os.path.basename(args.model),
                tokens=args.tokens,
                ms_per_token=ms_per_token,
                tok_s=tok_s,
                blk_tok_s=blk_tok_s,
                wall_s=dt,
                hit_rate=tier.tel.hit_rate,
                bytes_fetched=tier.tel.bytes_fetched,
                hot_bytes=args.hot_bytes,
                bank_mode=args.bank_mode,
                fixed_path_pinned=args.pin_fixed_path,
            ), f, indent=1)

    ex.free()
    tier.free()
    if fpr:
        fpr.free()
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


===== sabah/tools/run_model.py (pass 1) =====

"""
Sabah - full-model sequential MoE runner.

Runs all 48 MoE blocks sequentially per token, on real weights, using
Sabah's own runtime (no llama.cpp). This is the "Yol 1" path:

    * Fixed path (attention/router/embedding/head) pinned in VRAM once
    * Expert bank read from disk/RAM on-demand via ExpertBank
    * Hot tier (VRAM) + warm tier (pinned RAM) hide transfer latency
    * Block L's router output feeds block L+1 - sequential chain

This does NOT include attention/embedding/head arithmetic (those tensors
are pinned but not executed here). It measures the MoE block throughput
which is the dominant cost for this MoE model.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import sys
import time
import argparse
import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "..", ".."))

from sabah.core.model_inspector import inspect_model
from sabah.runtime import rt
from sabah.runtime.expert_bank import ExpertBank
from sabah.runtime.hot_tier import HotTier
from sabah.runtime.executor import MoEExecutor
from sabah.runtime.residency import FixedPathResident


def load_trace(trace_dir: str, n_blocks: int, topk: int, n_tokens: int,
               n_experts: int) -> dict:
    """Load routing ids for all blocks: {block: [n_tokens, topk]}.

    Missing trace files are filled with random top-k ids (uniform over
    experts) so the runner works on any machine without a pre-captured trace.
    """
    traces = {}
    rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
    for b in range(n_blocks):
        p = os.path.join(trace_dir, "ffn_moe_topk_L%d.i32" % b)
        if os.path.exists(p):
            a = np.fromfile(p, dtype=np.int32)
            if a.size % topk:
                raise ValueError("%s holds %d ids, not a multiple of top-%d"
                                 % (os.path.basename(p), a.size, topk))
            traces[b] = a.reshape(-1, topk)[:n_tokens]
        else:
            traces[b] = rng.integers(0, n_experts, size=(n_tokens, topk),
                                     dtype=np.int32)
    return traces


def main(argv=None):
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="sabah run-model")
    ap.add_argument("--model", required=True, help="Path to shard 1 .gguf")
    ap.add_argument("--trace", required=True, help="Directory with ffn_moe_topk_L*.i32")
    ap.add_argument("--tokens", type=int, default=500, help="Tokens to measure")
    ap.add_argument("--warmup", type=int, default=10, help="Warmup tokens (not timed)")
    ap.add_argument("--hot-bytes", type=int, default=536870912,
                    help="VRAM hot tier bytes (default 512 MB)")
    ap.add_argument("--bank-mode", default="mmap", choices=["mmap", "ram"],
                    help="mmap: page-cache backed. ram: copy block experts into pinned RAM.")
    ap.add_argument("--prefetch-slots", type=int, default=0,
                    help="Prefetch reserve slots per block (0 = off)")
    ap.add_argument("--pin-fixed-path", action="store_true",
                    help="Pin fixed path tensors into VRAM (piece C)")
    ap.add_argument("--json-out", default="", help="Write results JSON")
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)

    if not rt.available() or rt.device_count() < 1:
        print("CUDA runtime unavailable."); return 2
    rt.check(rt.lib().sabah_rt_init(0), "rt_init")

    prof = inspect_model(args.model)
    if not prof.supported:
        print("unsupported model"); return 2

    # ---- pin fixed path into VRAM (piece C) -------------------------------
    fpr = None
    if args.pin_fixed_path:
        fpr = FixedPathResident(prof)
        fpr.load()
        print("fixed path: %.3f GB pinned in VRAM (%d tensors)"
              % (fpr.resident_bytes / 1e9, len(fpr._ptrs)))

    # ---- build expert bank --------------------------------------------------
    bank = ExpertBank(prof, mode=args.bank_mode,
                      blocks=list(range(prof.n_blocks)))

    # ---- build hot tier -----------------------------------------------------
    tier = HotTier(bank, capacity_bytes=args.hot_bytes,
                   blocks=list(range(prof.n_blocks)),
                   prefetch_slots=args.prefetch_slots)
    print("hot tier : %.3f GB VRAM, %d slots/block"
          % (tier.capacity_bytes / 1e9, tier.slots_per_block))

    # ---- build executor -----------------------------------------------------
    ex = MoEExecutor(bank, tier, max_used=prof.n_experts_used)

    # ---- load routing traces ------------------------------------------------
    traces = load_trace(args.trace, prof.n_blocks,
                        prof.n_experts_used, args.tokens + args.warmup,
                        prof.n_experts)

    # ---- uniform routing weights -------------------------------------------
    w = np.full(prof.n_experts_used, 1.0 / prof.n_experts_used, dtype=np.float32)

    # ---- d_model input ------------------------------------------------------
    x = (np.random.default_rng(7).standard_normal(prof.d_model)
         .astype(np.float32) * 0.05)

    # ---- warmup (not timed) ------------------------------------------------
    print("warming up %d tokens..." % args.warmup)
    for t in range(args.warmup):
        for b in range(prof.n_blocks):
            ex.run_block(b, traces[b][t].tolist(), w)
    ex.sync()
    tier.tel.reset()

    # ---- timed run ----------------------------------------------------------
    print("running %d tokens x %d blocks..." % (args.tokens, prof.n_blocks))
    rt.check(rt.lib().sabah_sync_all(), "pre-timer sync")
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    for t in range(args.tokens):
        for b in range(prof.n_blocks):
            ex.run_block(b, traces[b][args.warmup + t].tolist(), w)
    ex.sync()
    dt = time.perf_counter() - t0

    # ---- results ------------------------------------------------------------
    n_blocks_executed = args.tokens * prof.n_blocks
    ms_per_token = 1e3 * dt / args.tokens
    tok_s = args.tokens / dt
    blk_tok_s = n_blocks_executed / dt

    print("=" * 60)
    print("RESULT: %.3f ms/token  ->  %.1f tok/s  (%.1f block-tok/s)"
          % (ms_per_token, tok_s, blk_tok_s))
    print("tokens : %d" % args.tokens)
    print("blocks : %d x %d = %d block executions"
          % (prof.n_blocks, args.tokens, n_blocks_executed))
    print("wall   : %.3f s" % dt)
    print("hit rate: %.4f" % tier.tel.hit_rate)
    print("bytes fetched: %.2f MB total"
          % (tier.tel.bytes_fetched / 1e6))
    print("=" * 60)

    if args.json_out:
        import json
        with open(args.json_out, "w") as f:
            json.dump(dict(
                model=os.path.basename(args.model),
                block_count=prof.n_blocks,
                tokens=args.tokens,
                ms_per_token=ms_per_token,
                tok_s=tok_s,
                blk_tok_s=blk_tok_s,
                wall_s=dt,
                hit_rate=tier.tel.hit_rate,
                bytes_fetched=tier.tel.bytes_fetched,
                hot_bytes=args.hot_bytes,
                bank_mode=args.bank_mode,
                fixed_path_pinned=args.pin_fixed_path,
                fixed_path_bytes=fpr.resident_bytes if fpr else 0,
            ), f, indent=1)
        print("wrote %s" % args.json_out)

    ex.free()
    tier.free()
    if fpr:
        fpr.free()
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


===== sabah/tools/stream.py (pass 1) =====

"""
Sabah Accelerator - the shipped streaming path, driven by one command.

Everything the bridge needs is an environment variable, and every number this
module reports is parsed out of the driver's own output, out of the runtime's own
counters, or out of the per-step times the driver writes next to them. Nothing
here projects, estimates or simulates: the planner owns projections, and they are
labelled as such there.

    sabah run    run the streaming decode once, per regime
    sabah matrix run a few configurations and write results/matrix.json
    sabah gate   run the correctness switches as an automated pass/fail gate

A generation has three regimes and `run` reports all three, because averaging
them is how this project mis-quoted its own speed for a while:

* **fill**  - step 0, the prompt, whose cost is the tier's cold fill (12.68 GB
              at ~1.1 GB/s on the reference rig), not prompt processing.
* **warm-up** - the next ~128 decodes, while the tier is still discovering its
              working set (~70 MB/token of disk there).
* **steady state** - after that the working set is resident (3 MB/token) and the
              token is the no-disk floor, ~67 ms on the reference rig.

The driver (`llama-sabah-diag.exe`) is patch 0002 of the llama.cpp integration;
its path is `--diag` or `SABAH_DIAG_EXE`. The runtime is `--rt` or
`SABAH_RT_LIB`, defaulting to the library built in this tree.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import struct
import subprocess
import sys
import time

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(HERE, "..", ".."))

from sabah.tools import clocks  # noqa: E402

DEFAULT_RT = os.path.join(ROOT, "sabah", "runtime", "cuda", "sabah_rt.dll")

# The placement requirements (see patches/llama.cpp/README.md). Without both of
# these the expert ops never reach the bridge.
BASE_ENV = {
    "SABAH_LLAMA": "1",
    "GGML_OP_OFFLOAD_MIN_BATCH": "1",
    "SABAH_LLAMA_DIRECT_IO": "1",
    "SABAH_LLAMA_IO_DEPTH": "8",
    "SABAH_LLAMA_TRACE": "1",
}

# The reproduction flags from the README, and nothing else.
BASE_FLAGS = [
    "-dev", "CUDA0", "-ngl", "99", "--cpu-moe",
    "-ot", r"\.per_layer_token_embd\.weight=CPU",
]

# What the driver and the runtime write into the run directory. They are wiped
# before every run: a file left by an earlier configuration - or by a run that
# died before finishing - must never be read back as this run's numbers.
RUN_OUTPUTS = ("step_ms.f64", "prompt_tokens.i32", "fed.i32", "argmax.i32",
               "logits.f32", "prompt.txt", "run.json", "sabah_status_live.json")


def clean_run_dir(out_dir):
    """Remove the transient outputs of a previous run from `out_dir`."""
    for name in RUN_OUTPUTS:
        p = os.path.join(out_dir, name)
        if os.path.exists(p):
            try:
                os.remove(p)
            except OSError:
                pass


class RunError(RuntimeError):
    pass


def sha256(path, limit=None):
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    n = 0
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        while True:
            b = f.read(1 << 20)
            if not b:
                break
            h.update(b)
            n += len(b)
            if limit and n >= limit:
                break
    return h.hexdigest()


def _num(text, pattern, cast=float):
    m = re.search(pattern, text)
    return cast(m.group(1)) if m else None


def parse_driver(text):
    """Everything the driver and the runtime printed, as plain numbers."""
    out = {}
    # Step 0 is the prefill: it must not be counted or timed as a decoded token.
    steps = [float(m.group(2)) for m in
             re.finditer(r"prompt step (\d+) .*?([\d.]+) ms", text)
             if int(m.group(1)) >= 1]
    out["n_decodes"] = len(steps)
    if steps:
        srt = sorted(steps)
        n = len(srt)
        med = srt[n // 2] if n % 2 else (srt[n // 2 - 1] + srt[n // 2]) / 2.0
        out["decode_ms_median"] = med
        out["decode_ms_sum"] = sum(steps)
        out["decode_ms_per_token"] = sum(steps) / n
        out["decode_tok_per_s"] = 1000.0 / out["decode_ms_per_token"]

    def grab(label, pattern, cast=float):
        v = _num(text, pattern, cast)
        if v is not None:
            out[label] = v

    grab("calls", r"SABAH_DIAG calls=(\d+)", int)
    grab("tokens", r"SABAH_DIAG calls=\d+ tokens=(\d+)", int)
    grab("lookups", r"SABAH_DIAG calls=\d+ tokens=\d+ lookups=(\d+)", int)
    grab("overflow", r"overflow=(\d+)", int)
    grab("verify_ok", r"verify_ok=(\d+)", int)
    grab("verify_fail", r"verify_fail=(\d+)", int)
    grab("unbuffered_reads", r"SABAH_DISK unbuffered_reads=(\d+)", int)
    grab("read_bytes", r"SABAH_DISK unbuffered_reads=\d+ bytes=(\d+)", int)
    grab("waves", r"waves=(\d+)", int)
    grab("mapped_fallbacks", r"mapped_fallbacks=(\d+)", int)
    grab("staging_bytes", r"staging_bytes=(\d+)", int)
    grab("io_overlap", r"SABAH_IO read_ms=[\d.]+ wave_ms=[\d.]+ overlap=([\d.]+)")
    grab("phase_ids_ms", r"SABAH_PHASE calls=\d+ ids=([\d.]+)ms")
    grab("phase_fetch_ms", r"ids=[\d.]+ms fetch=([\d.]+)ms")
    grab("phase_wait_ms", r"fetch=[\d.]+ms wait=([\d.]+)ms")
    grab("phase_slot_ms", r"wait=[\d.]+ms slot=([\d.]+)ms")
    grab("phase_ptr_ms", r"slot=[\d.]+ms ptr=([\d.]+)ms")
    grab("phase_prime_ms", r"ptr=[\d.]+ms prime=([\d.]+)ms")
    grab("id_reuse", r"SABAH_IDS reused=(\d+)", int)
    grab("id_group_max", r"SABAH_IDS reused=\d+ group_max=(\d+)", int)
    grab("id_check", r"SABAH_IDS reused=\d+ group_max=\d+ check=(\d+)", int)
    grab("id_check_ok", r"check=\d+ check_ok=(\d+)", int)
    grab("id_check_fail", r"check_ok=\d+ check_fail=(\d+)", int)
    # The exact cache counters come from the runtime's exit snapshot, not from
    # SABAH_LLAMA_STATUS_FILE: the status file is rewritten after every expert
    # call, which costs 144 synchronous writes per token and poisons any timing
    # taken in the same pass (~2 ms per call here, ~0.3 s per token).
    i = text.find("SABAH_SNAPSHOT ")
    if i >= 0:
        j = text.find("\n", i)
        try:
            snap = json.loads(text[i + len("SABAH_SNAPSHOT "):j if j >= 0 else len(text)])
        except ValueError:
            snap = None
        if isinstance(snap, dict):
            out["snapshot"] = snap
            for k in ("calls", "tokens", "lookups", "hits", "misses", "evictions",
                      "overflow", "resident_bytes", "bytes_fetched",
                      "verify_ok", "verify_fail", "empty_calls"):
                if k in snap:
                    out[k] = snap[k]
    phases = [out.get(k) for k in ("phase_ids_ms", "phase_fetch_ms", "phase_slot_ms",
                                   "phase_ptr_ms", "phase_prime_ms")]
    if all(v is not None for v in phases) and out.get("n_decodes"):
        out["phase_sum_ms_per_call"] = sum(phases) / max(out["calls"] or 1, 1)
    return out


def status(path):
    if not path or not os.path.exists(path):
        return {}
    try:
        with open(path, "r") as f:
            t = f.read().strip()
        return json.loads(t) if t and t != "null" else {}
    except (ValueError, OSError):
        return {}


def step_ms(out_dir):
    """The driver's per-step wall times. Index 0 is the prompt (the fill)."""
    path = os.path.join(out_dir, "step_ms.f64")
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        return []
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        raw = f.read()
    n = len(raw) // 8
    return list(struct.unpack("<%dd" % n, raw[: n * 8]))


def prompt_tokens(out_dir):
    """The ids the driver actually fed. A templated prompt is not a short one:
    `-p "Hello"` is 53 tokens, and `tokens=` in the trace line is not this."""
    path = os.path.join(out_dir, "prompt_tokens.i32")
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        return []
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        raw = f.read()
    n = len(raw) // 4
    return list(struct.unpack("<%di" % n, raw[: n * 4]))


def _median(xs):
    s = sorted(xs)
    n = len(s)
    if n == 0:
        return None
    return s[n // 2] if n % 2 else 0.5 * (s[n // 2 - 1] + s[n // 2])


def regimes(out_dir, warm_tokens=128, fill_ms=None):
    """Split one run into fill / warm-up / steady state.

    The split is not cosmetic. `-n 32` minus `-n 8` differencing (the decode
    budget method) lands inside the warm-up, so it prices the marginal token of
    a generation that has just started - 190.9 ms/token on the reference rig
    against a 67 ms steady state. Quoting either one alone is a mis-measurement.
    """
    ms = step_ms(out_dir)
    if len(ms) < 2:
        return {}
    fill = ms[0] if fill_ms is None else fill_ms
    dec = ms[1:]
    warm = dec[:warm_tokens]
    steady = dec[warm_tokens:]
    out = {
        "fill_ms": fill,
        "decodes": len(dec),
        "warmup": {"n": len(warm), "median_ms": _median(warm),
                   "mean_ms": (sum(warm) / len(warm)) if warm else None},
    }
    if steady:
        m = _median(steady)
        out["steady"] = {"n": len(steady), "median_ms": m,
                         "mean_ms": sum(steady) / len(steady),
                         "tok_per_s": (1000.0 / m) if m else None}
    total = sum(dec)
    if total > 0:
        out["decode_only_tok_per_s"] = 1000.0 * len(dec) / total
        out["end_to_end_tok_per_s"] = 1000.0 * len(dec) / (total + fill)
    return out


def run(model, prompt="Hello", tokens=32, context=512, threads=8,
        hot_bytes="9.8e9", diag="", rt="", extra_env=None, extra_flags=None,
        out_dir=None, timeout=1800, quiet=False, counters=False):
    """Run the streaming decode once. Returns the measured record.

    The cache counters (hits, misses, evictions, resident bytes) come from the
    runtime's exit snapshot, which is exact, so nothing here needs the status
    file. `counters=True` still sets `SABAH_LLAMA_STATUS_FILE` - the live-server
    switch - and the runtime rewrites it after *every* expert call: 144
    synchronous file writes per token here, worth ~0.3 s per token. Never time a
    run with it set.
    """
    diag = diag or os.environ.get("SABAH_DIAG_EXE", "")
    if not diag:
        raise RunError("no driver: pass --diag <llama-sabah-diag.exe> or set SABAH_DIAG_EXE\n"
                       "(build it from patches/llama.cpp/0002-sabah-diag-capture-tool.patch)")
    if not os.path.exists(diag):
        raise RunError("driver not found: %s" % diag)
    rt = rt or os.environ.get("SABAH_RT_LIB", "") or DEFAULT_RT
    if not os.path.exists(rt):
        raise RunError("runtime not found: %s (build it, or pass --rt)" % rt)
    if not os.path.exists(model):
        raise RunError("model not found: %s" % model)

    out_dir = out_dir or os.path.join(ROOT, "results", "_run")
    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
    status_path = os.path.join(out_dir, "sabah_status_live.json")

    env = dict(os.environ)
    env.update(BASE_ENV)
    env["SABAH_RT_LIB"] = os.path.abspath(rt)
    env["SABAH_LLAMA_HOT_BYTES"] = str(hot_bytes)
    if counters:
        env["SABAH_LLAMA_STATUS_FILE"] = status_path
    else:
        env.pop("SABAH_LLAMA_STATUS_FILE", None)
    env["SABAH_DIAG_OUT"] = out_dir
    env["SABAH_LLAMA_PREFETCH"] = "0"
    env.pop("SABAH_LLAMA_LAYER_MERGE", None)
    if extra_env:
        env.update({k: str(v) for k, v in extra_env.items()})

    argv = [os.path.abspath(diag), "-m", os.path.abspath(model)]
    argv += BASE_FLAGS
    argv += ["-t", str(threads), "-c", str(context), "-n", str(tokens), "-p", prompt]
    if extra_flags:
        argv += [str(f) for f in extra_flags]

    # A status file or a step stream left by an earlier run in this directory must
    # never be read back as this run's output.
    clean_run_dir(out_dir)

    t0 = time.time()
    clock_sampler = clocks.ClockSampler(interval=0.5)
    clock_sampler.start()
    proc = subprocess.run(argv, env=env, capture_output=True, text=True,
                          encoding="utf-8", errors="replace", timeout=timeout)
    wall = time.time() - t0
    rec = parse_driver(proc.stdout + proc.stderr)
    clock_rec = clock_sampler.stop().record(decode_ms=rec.get("decode_ms_sum"))
    rec["wall_s"] = wall
    rec["exit_code"] = proc.returncode
    st = status(status_path) if counters else {}
    if st:
        rec["resident_bytes"] = st.get("resident_bytes")
        rec["misses"] = st.get("misses")
        rec["hits"] = st.get("hits")
        rec["evictions"] = st.get("evictions")
        rec["cached_bytes_fetched"] = st.get("bytes_fetched")
        rec["status_verify_ok"] = st.get("verify_ok")
        rec["status_verify_fail"] = st.get("verify_fail")
    rec["hot_bytes"] = str(hot_bytes)
    rec["model"] = os.path.abspath(model)
    rec["tokens_requested"] = tokens
    rec["prompt"] = prompt
    rec["driver"] = os.path.abspath(diag)
    rec["runtime"] = os.path.abspath(rt)
    rec["counters_enabled"] = bool(counters)
    rec["clocks"] = clock_rec
    rec["clock_valid"] = clock_rec.get("valid")
    rec["clock_invalid_reason"] = clock_rec.get("reason")
    rec["machine"] = clocks.machine_state()
    pt = prompt_tokens(out_dir)
    if pt:
        rec["prompt_tokens_count"] = len(pt)
        rec["prompt_tokens"] = pt
    # The driver writes the per-step times next to its other output; they are the
    # only way to see that a run reached the steady state at all.
    reg = regimes(out_dir, fill_ms=rec.get("prefill_ms"))
    if reg:
        rec["regimes"] = reg
        rec["fill_ms"] = reg.get("fill_ms")
        for k in ("decode_only_tok_per_s", "end_to_end_tok_per_s"):
            if reg.get(k) is not None:
                rec[k] = reg[k]
        if reg.get("steady"):
            rec["steady_ms_median"] = reg["steady"]["median_ms"]
            rec["steady_tok_per_s"] = reg["steady"]["tok_per_s"]
    if rec.get("read_bytes") and rec.get("n_decodes"):
        rec["read_bytes_per_decoded_token"] = rec["read_bytes"] / rec["n_decodes"]
    if not quiet:
        print_breakdown(rec)
    return rec


def print_breakdown(r):
    print("  model        %s" % (os.path.basename(r.get("model") or "") or r.get("driver", "")))
    print("  exit code    %d   wall %.1f s" % (r.get("exit_code", -1), r.get("wall_s", 0.0)))
    reg = r.get("regimes") or {}
    if reg.get("fill_ms") is not None:
        print("  fill         %.1f s before the first token (%s prompt tokens)"
              % (reg["fill_ms"] / 1000.0, r.get("prompt_tokens_count", "?")))
    if reg.get("warmup"):
        w = reg["warmup"]
        print("  warm-up      %d tokens, median %.1f ms  (the tier is still filling)"
              % (w["n"], w["median_ms"] or 0.0))
    if reg.get("steady"):
        s = reg["steady"]
        print("  steady       %d tokens, median %.1f ms = %.2f tok/s  (working set resident)"
              % (s["n"], s["median_ms"] or 0.0, s["tok_per_s"] or 0.0))
    if r.get("decode_only_tok_per_s") is not None:
        print("  overall      %.2f tok/s over the decodes, %.2f tok/s including the fill"
              % (r["decode_only_tok_per_s"], r.get("end_to_end_tok_per_s") or 0.0))
    if r.get("read_bytes") is not None:
        print("  disk         %.2f GB read, %.1f MB per decoded token (the fill included)"
              % (r["read_bytes"] / 1e9, (r.get("read_bytes_per_decoded_token") or 0) / 1e6))
    if r.get("resident_bytes") is not None:
        print("  cache        %s hits, %s misses, %s evictions, resident %.2f GB of %s"
              % (r.get("hits"), r.get("misses"), r.get("evictions"),
                 (r.get("resident_bytes") or 0) / 1e9, r.get("hot_bytes")))
    ph = ["phase_ids_ms", "phase_fetch_ms", "phase_wait_ms", "phase_slot_ms",
          "phase_ptr_ms", "phase_prime_ms"]
    if all(r.get(k) is not None for k in ph):
        print("  phases       " + "  ".join("%s=%.1fms" % (k.replace("phase_", "").replace("_ms", ""), r[k])
                                           for k in ph))
    print("  checks       verify %s/%s, mapped_fallbacks=%s, overflow=%s"
          % (r.get("verify_ok"), r.get("verify_fail"),
             r.get("mapped_fallbacks"), r.get("overflow")))
    if r.get("clocks"):
        print("  " + clocks.format_steady(r["clocks"]))
        if r["clocks"].get("valid") is False:
            print("  CLOCK INVALID: %s" % r["clocks"].get("reason"))


def environment_fingerprint(model, rt, diag):
    fp = {"generated": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S"),
          "python": sys.version.split()[0]}
    for key, path in (("runtime_sha256", rt), ("driver_sha256", diag)):
        try:
            fp[key] = sha256(path)
        except OSError:
            fp[key] = None
    try:
        st = os.stat(model)
        fp["model"] = {"path": os.path.abspath(model), "bytes": st.st_size,
                       "mtime": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S", time.localtime(st.st_mtime))}
    except OSError:
        fp["model"] = {"path": model}
    head = os.path.join(ROOT, ".git", "HEAD")
    fp["repo_head"] = _git_head()
    return fp


def _git_head():
    try:
        out = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", ROOT, "rev-parse", "HEAD"],
                             capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
        return out.stdout.strip() or None
    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
        return None


MATRIX = [
    # label, env overrides, extra flags, tokens (or None for the default)
    ("tier-9.8G", {}, [], None),
    ("tier-1G", {"SABAH_LLAMA_HOT_BYTES": "1e9"}, [], None),
    ("tier-9.8G-verify", {"SABAH_LLAMA_VERIFY_BYTES": "1",
                          "SABAH_LLAMA_VERIFY_LIMIT": "2000",
                          "SABAH_LLAMA_ID_CHECK": "1"}, [], 8),
    # The steady state only exists after the tier has learned its working set
    # (~130 decodes on the reference rig), so one row has to be long.
    ("tier-9.8G-steady", {}, [], 256),
]


def matrix(model, diag="", rt="", tokens=32, tokens_verify=8, out="",
           hot_bytes="9.8e9", prompt="Hello", threads=8, context=512):
    """Run the configurations the README quotes, and write them as one JSON."""
    recs = []
    for label, env, flags, row_tokens in MATRIX:
        n = row_tokens or (tokens_verify if "verify" in label else tokens)
        print("-- %s (n=%d)" % (label, n))
        rec = run(model, prompt=prompt, tokens=n, context=context, threads=threads,
                  hot_bytes=env.get("SABAH_LLAMA_HOT_BYTES", hot_bytes),
                  diag=diag, rt=rt, extra_env=env, extra_flags=flags)
        rec["label"] = label
        rec["model"] = model
        recs.append(rec)
        print()
    doc = {"format": "sabah.results.matrix/1",
           "environment": environment_fingerprint(model, os.path.abspath(rt or DEFAULT_RT), diag),
           "runs": recs}
    if out:
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(out)), exist_ok=True)
        with open(out, "w") as f:
            json.dump(doc, f, indent=2, sort_keys=True)
            f.write("\n")
        print("wrote %s" % out)
    return doc


def gate_checks(rec):
    """The pass/fail checks a completed run must satisfy. Pure, so it is tested."""
    return [
        ("the bridge executed expert ops", (rec.get("calls") or 0) > 0, rec.get("calls")),
        ("every served byte matched the GGUF", rec.get("verify_ok", 0) > 0 and
         rec.get("verify_fail", 0) == 0, "%s ok / %s fail" % (rec.get("verify_ok"),
                                                              rec.get("verify_fail"))),
        ("the id reuse is byte for byte", rec.get("id_check_ok", 0) > 0 and
         rec.get("id_check_fail", 0) == 0,
         "%s ok / %s fail" % (rec.get("id_check_ok"), rec.get("id_check_fail"))),
        ("no mapped fallback", (rec.get("mapped_fallbacks") or 0) == 0,
         rec.get("mapped_fallbacks")),
        ("decode finished", rec.get("exit_code") == 0, rec.get("exit_code")),
    ]


def gate_notes(rec):
    """Counters that are reported but never gated.

    `overflow` is over-budget allocations, not bad expert ids: when every
    candidate victim is still referenced by the call in flight, the tier
    allocates past its budget rather than dangle a pointer. That is documented
    behaviour and the byte comparison in gate_checks is what proves the routed
    result stayed exact. This function exists because an earlier version of the
    gate hard-failed on the counter under the name "an expert id escaped the
    model", which was wrong about what the counter means.
    """
    return [("over-budget allocations", rec.get("overflow"))]


def gate(model, diag="", rt="", tokens=8, hot_bytes="9.8e9",
         verify_limit=2000, prompt="Hello", threads=8, context=512):
    """The correctness switches, as a pass/fail gate."""
    print("-- gate: byte-compare what the runtime serves, and prove the id reuse")
    rec = run(model, prompt=prompt, tokens=tokens, context=context, threads=threads,
              hot_bytes=hot_bytes, diag=diag, rt=rt,
              extra_env={"SABAH_LLAMA_VERIFY_BYTES": "1",
                         "SABAH_LLAMA_VERIFY_LIMIT": str(verify_limit),
                         "SABAH_LLAMA_ID_CHECK": "1"})
    checks = gate_checks(rec)
    notes = gate_notes(rec)
    print()
    ok = True
    for label, good, detail in checks:
        print("  [%s] %-36s %s" % ("ok" if good else "!!", label, detail))
        ok = ok and good
    for label, detail in notes:
        print("  [--] %-36s %s (documented; correctness is the checks above)"
              % (label, detail))
    print()
    print("gate: %s" % ("PASS" if ok else "FAIL"))
    return 0 if ok else 1


===== tools/_cnt.py (pass 1) =====

import sqlite3, sys
db = sys.argv[1]
c = sqlite3.connect(db); c.text_factory = bytes
for t in ["CUPTI_ACTIVITY_KIND_RUNTIME", "CUPTI_ACTIVITY_KIND_KERNEL", "CUPTI_ACTIVITY_KIND_MEMCPY", "CUPTI_ACTIVITY_KIND_SYNCHRONIZATION"]:
    try:
        n = c.execute("select count(*) from %s" % t).fetchone()[0]
        print(t, n)
    except Exception as e:
        print(t, "MISSING")


===== tools/_kern.py (pass 1) =====

import sqlite3, sys, collections
db = sys.argv[1]
c = sqlite3.connect(db); c.text_factory = bytes

def names():
    return {k: v.decode("utf-8", "replace") for k, v in c.execute("select id,value from StringIds")}

s = names()

print("=== processes / streams ===")
try:
    for row in c.execute("select * from PROCESSES"):
        print(row)
except Exception as e:
    print("PROCESSES err", e)

print("\n=== top runtime API by count ===")
try:
    for row in c.execute("select nameId, count(*), sum(end-start) from CUPTI_ACTIVITY_KIND_RUNTIME group by nameId order by count(*) desc limit 15"):
        print("%-45s n=%d sum_us=%.0f" % (s.get(row[0], "?"), row[1], row[2] / 1000))
except Exception as e:
    print("runtime err", e)

print("\n=== top kernels by count ===")
try:
    for row in c.execute("select demangledName, count(*), sum(end-start) from CUPTI_ACTIVITY_KIND_KERNEL group by demangledName order by count(*) desc limit 20"):
        nm = s.get(row[0], "?")
        print("%-55s n=%d sum_ms=%.1f" % (nm[:55], row[1], row[2] / 1e6))
except Exception as e:
    print("kernel err", e)

            n = c.execute("select count(*) from %s" % t).fetchone()[0]

===== tools/capture_to_trace.py (pass 1) =====

"""Convert sabah-diag capture blobs into the trace_locality i32 layout.

The diag writes ``<item>/cap/ffn_moe_topk_L<block>.bin``: i32, token-major rows
of ``top_k`` expert ids, appended in execution order (prefill positions first,
then one row per decode step). trace_locality wants one
``ffn_moe_topk_L<block>.i32`` per block with every item concatenated in manifest
order, so this is a concatenation, not a conversion.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import glob
import json
import os
import re


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--capture", required=True, help="SABAH_DIAG_OUT directory")
    ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--manifest", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--top-k", type=int, default=10)
    ap.add_argument("--tail-decode", action="store_true",
                    help="keep only the last n_predict rows of each item (decode)")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    order = []
    n_predict = {}
    with open(args.manifest, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        for line in f:
            line = line.strip()
            if line:
                e = json.loads(line)
                order.append(e["id"])
                n_predict[e["id"]] = int(e.get("n_predict", 64))

    os.makedirs(args.out, exist_ok=True)
    pat = re.compile(r"^ffn_moe_topk_L(\d+)\.bin$")
    totals: dict[int, int] = {}
    for item in order:
        cap = os.path.join(args.capture, item, "cap")
        if not os.path.isdir(cap):
            raise SystemExit("missing capture dir: %s" % cap)
        for name in sorted(os.listdir(cap)):
            m = pat.match(name)
            if not m:
                continue
            block = int(m.group(1))
            with open(os.path.join(cap, name), "rb") as src:
                data = src.read()
            if len(data) % (4 * args.top_k):
                raise SystemExit("%s: size %d is not a whole number of rows"
                                 % (name, len(data)))
            rows = len(data) // (4 * args.top_k)
            if args.tail_decode:
                keep = min(n_predict[item], rows)
                data = data[(rows - keep) * 4 * args.top_k:]
            dst = os.path.join(args.out, "ffn_moe_topk_L%d.i32" % block)
            with open(dst, "ab") as out:
                out.write(data)
            totals[block] = totals.get(block, 0) + len(data) // (4 * args.top_k)

    if not totals:
        raise SystemExit("no ffn_moe_topk blobs found under %s" % args.capture)
    if len(set(totals.values())) != 1:
        print("warning: blocks differ in steps: %s"
              % sorted(set(totals.values())))
    print("%d blocks, %d steps each, %d items -> %s"
          % (len(totals), next(iter(totals.values())), len(order), args.out))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


===== tools/clock_trace.py (pass 1) =====

"""1 s-resolution idle -> decode -> idle clock trace.

Catches the transition into the low-clock state (if it happens) and records the
machine settings that allow it: Windows power plan, HAGS, NVIDIA power
management registry values, display state.

    python tools/clock_trace.py --idle-before 20 --idle-after 40 --tokens 64

The trace is a CSV of every sample plus a JSON summary that names the first
sample below 2400 MHz and what preceded it.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
import time

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(HERE, ".."))
if ROOT not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, ROOT)

from sabah.tools import clocks, stream  # noqa: E402

DEFAULT_MODEL = (r"$DRIVE\Strata-data\models\IQ3_S"
                 r"\Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-GSQ-RCO-IQ3_S-00001-of-00002.gguf")
DEFAULT_DIAG = os.path.join(ROOT, "third_party", "llama.cpp", "build", "bin",
                            "llama-sabah-diag.exe")
LOW_MHZ = clocks.MIN_STEADY_SM_MHZ


def run_decode(args, out_dir, sampler):
    env = dict(os.environ)
    env.update(stream.BASE_ENV)
    env["SABAH_RT_LIB"] = os.path.abspath(args.rt)
    env["SABAH_LLAMA_HOT_BYTES"] = str(args.hot_bytes)
    env["SABAH_DIAG_OUT"] = out_dir
    env["SABAH_LLAMA_PREFETCH"] = "0"
    env.pop("SABAH_LLAMA_STATUS_FILE", None)
    argv = [os.path.abspath(args.diag), "-m", os.path.abspath(args.model)]
    argv += stream.BASE_FLAGS
    argv += ["-t", "8", "-c", str(args.context), "-n", str(args.tokens),
             "-p", args.prompt]
    stream.clean_run_dir(out_dir)
    log = os.path.join(out_dir, "driver.log")
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    with open(log, "w", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
        proc = subprocess.run(argv, env=env, stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
    t1 = time.monotonic()
    text = open(log, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").read()
    rec = stream.parse_driver(text)
    return {"exit_code": proc.returncode, "t0": t0, "t1": t1,
            "decode_ms_sum": rec.get("decode_ms_sum"),
            "decode_ms_median": rec.get("decode_ms_median"),
            "n_decodes": rec.get("n_decodes"),
            "read_bytes": rec.get("read_bytes")}


def analyse(samples, decode):
    out = {"low_threshold_mhz": LOW_MHZ, "first_low": None,
           "low_samples": 0, "min_sm": None, "min_at_s": None,
           "transitions": []}
    prev = None
    for t, s in samples:
        sm = s.get("sm_mhz")
        if sm is not None and (out["min_sm"] is None or sm < out["min_sm"]):
            out["min_sm"] = sm
            out["min_at_s"] = round(t, 2)
        state = "low" if (sm is not None and sm < LOW_MHZ) else "boost"
        if prev is not None and state != prev:
            out["transitions"].append({"t_s": round(t, 2), "to": state,
                                       "sm_mhz": sm, "pstate": s.get("pstate")})
        prev = state
        if state == "low":
            out["low_samples"] += 1
            if out["first_low"] is None:
                out["first_low"] = {"t_s": round(t, 2), "sm_mhz": sm,
                                    "pstate": s.get("pstate"),
                                    "power_w": s.get("power_w")}
    if decode:
        out["decode"] = {"start_s": round(decode["t0"], 2),
                         "end_s": round(decode["t1"], 2),
                         "duration_s": round(decode["t1"] - decode["t0"], 2)}
    return out


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--model", default=DEFAULT_MODEL)
    ap.add_argument("--diag", default=DEFAULT_DIAG)
    ap.add_argument("--rt", default=os.path.join(ROOT, "sabah", "runtime",
                                                 "cuda", "sabah_rt.dll"))
    ap.add_argument("--tokens", type=int, default=64)
    ap.add_argument("--context", type=int, default=512)
    ap.add_argument("--hot-bytes", default="6.0e9")
    ap.add_argument("--prompt", default="Hello")
    ap.add_argument("--idle-before", type=float, default=20.0)
    ap.add_argument("--idle-after", type=float, default=40.0)
    ap.add_argument("--label", default="idle-decode-idle")
    ap.add_argument("--out-root", default=os.path.join(ROOT, "results",
                                                       "phase0", "clock-trace"))
    args = ap.parse_args()

    out_dir = os.path.join(args.out_root, args.label)
    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)

    sampler = clocks.ClockSampler(interval=1.0)
    sampler.start()
    started = time.monotonic()

    print(f"machine: {json.dumps(clocks.machine_state())}")
    print(f"idle {args.idle_before:.0f}s ...")
    time.sleep(args.idle_before)
    print(f"decode {args.tokens} tokens ...")
    dec = run_decode(args, out_dir, sampler)
    print(f"  exit={dec['exit_code']} decodes={dec['n_decodes']} "
          f"median={dec['decode_ms_median']} ms  "
          f"({dec['t1'] - dec['t0']:.1f}s wall)")
    print(f"idle {args.idle_after:.0f}s ...")
    time.sleep(args.idle_after)
    sampler.stop()

    samples = [(round(t - started, 2), s) for t, s in sampler.samples]
    dec["t0"] -= started
    dec["t1"] -= started
    summary = analyse(samples, dec)
    summary["machine"] = clocks.machine_state()
    summary["decode"] = dec
    summary["label"] = args.label
    summary["idle_before_s"] = args.idle_before
    summary["idle_after_s"] = args.idle_after

    csv_path = os.path.join(out_dir, "clocks.csv")
    with open(csv_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write("t_s,sm_mhz,mem_mhz,pstate,power_w,temp_c,util_pct,throttle\n")
        for t, s in samples:
            f.write(f"{t},{s['sm_mhz']},{s['mem_mhz']},{s['pstate']},"
                    f"{s['power_w']},{s['temp_c']},{s['util_pct']},"
                    f"{s['throttle']}\n")
    json_path = os.path.join(out_dir, "trace.json")
    with open(json_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(summary, f, indent=2, sort_keys=True)
        f.write("\n")

    same = [s for _, s in samples]
    print(f"\n{'t':>6} {'SM':>6} {'mem':>6} {'P':>3} {'W':>6} {'util':>5}")
    for t, s in samples:
        mark = ""
        if dec["t0"] <= t <= dec["t1"]:
            mark = " decode"
        print(f"{t:6.1f} {s['sm_mhz']:6.0f} {s['mem_mhz']:6.0f} "
              f"{s['pstate']:>3} {s['power_w']:6.1f} {s['util_pct']:5.0f}{mark}")
    print(f"\nmin SM {summary['min_sm']:.0f} MHz at {summary['min_at_s']}s, "
          f"low samples {summary['low_samples']}/{len(same)}")
    print(f"first low: {summary['first_low']}")
    print(f"transitions: {summary['transitions']}")
    print(f"wrote {csv_path}\n      {json_path}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())


===== tools/clocks_summary.py (pass 1) =====

import csv, sys, statistics, json, os

path = sys.argv[1]
rows = []
with open(path, newline="") as f:
    for r in csv.DictReader(f):
        try:
            rows.append({
                "t": float(r["t_s"]),
                "sm": float(r["sm_mhz"]),
                "mem": float(r["mem_mhz"]),
                "pstate": r["pstate"].strip(),
                "pw": float(r["power_w"]),
                "temp": float(r["temp_c"]),
                "util": float(r["util_pct"]),
                "thr": r["throttle"].strip(),
            })
        except (ValueError, KeyError):
            pass

def win(lo, hi):
    return [r for r in rows if lo <= r["t"] < hi]

def summ(name, w):
    if not w:
        print(f"{name}: no samples")
        return
    print(f"{name}: n={len(w)}  sm {min(x['sm'] for x in w):.0f}/{statistics.median(x['sm'] for x in w):.0f}/{max(x['sm'] for x in w):.0f}"
          f"  mem {min(x['mem'] for x in w):.0f}/{statistics.median(x['mem'] for x in w):.0f}/{max(x['mem'] for x in w):.0f}"
          f"  pw {min(x['pw'] for x in w):.1f}/{statistics.median(x['pw'] for x in w):.1f}/{max(x['pw'] for x in w):.1f}W"
          f"  util {statistics.median(x['util'] for x in w):.0f}%  temp {statistics.median(x['temp'] for x in w):.0f}C"
          f"  pstate {sorted(set(x['pstate'] for x in w))}  throttle {sorted(set(x['thr'] for x in w))}")

print("total samples", len(rows), "span %.1fs" % (rows[-1]["t"] if rows else 0))
summ("idle(0-1s)", win(0, 1))
summ("fill(1-13s)", win(1, 13))
summ("steady(13-40s)", win(13, 40))
summ("steady(20-40s)", win(20, 40))

d = os.path.dirname(path)
mj = os.path.join(d, "measurement.json")
if os.path.exists(mj):
    m = json.load(open(mj))
    print("steady_ms_median=%.3f  tok/s=%.3f  fill_s=%.2f  warm_ms=%.3f" % (
        m.get("steady_ms_median", 0), m.get("steady_tok_per_s", 0),
        m.get("fill_s", 0), m.get("warm_ms_median", 0) or 0))


===== tools/cmp_phase2.py (pass 1) =====

import json, struct
a = open(r"results\phase0\baseline-n256\argmax.i32", "rb").read()
b = open(r"results\phase0\phase2-tile1\argmax.i32", "rb").read()
print("len", len(a), len(b), "equal", a == b)
if a != b:
    A = struct.unpack("<%di" % (len(a) // 4), a)
    B = struct.unpack("<%di" % (len(b) // 4), b)
    diffs = [(i, A[i], B[i]) for i in range(min(len(A), len(B))) if A[i] != B[i]]
    print("diffs", len(diffs), diffs[:10])
m1 = json.load(open(r"results\phase0\baseline-n256\measurement.json"))
m2 = json.load(open(r"results\phase0\phase2-tile1\measurement.json"))
for k in ("steady_ms_median", "steady_tok_per_s", "exit_code", "memory"):
    print(k, m1.get(k), "->", m2.get(k))


===== tools/compare_runs.py (pass 1) =====

"""Byte-exact comparison of two diagnostic runs.

    python tools/compare_runs.py results/phase0/run-a results/phase0/run-b

Checks every file the diag writes for decoding drift (fed.i32, argmax.i32,
logits.f32) and reports the first differing byte plus the surrounding values,
so a mismatch can be attributed to a real numeric difference instead of a
generic FAIL.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import os
import sys

import numpy as np

FILES = ("fed.i32", "argmax.i32", "logits.f32")


def first_diff(a, b, dtype, name):
    n = min(a.size, b.size)
    if a.size != b.size:
        return f"{name}: size differs {a.size} vs {b.size}"
    idx = np.flatnonzero(a[:n] != b[:n])
    if idx.size == 0:
        return None
    i = int(idx[0])
    va, vb = a[i], b[i]
    if dtype == np.int32:
        detail = f"value {va} vs {vb}"
    else:
        import struct
        detail = (f"value {va!r} vs {vb!r}, bits "
                  f"0x{struct.unpack('<I', struct.pack('<f', float(va)))[0]:08x} vs "
                  f"0x{struct.unpack('<I', struct.pack('<f', float(vb)))[0]:08x}")
    return f"{name}: first diff at element {i} of {n} ({detail}); {idx.size} differ"


def load(path, dtype):
    if not os.path.exists(path):
        return None
    return np.fromfile(path, dtype=dtype)


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("a")
    ap.add_argument("b")
    ap.add_argument("--files", default=",".join(FILES))
    args = ap.parse_args()
    ok = True
    for name in [f.strip() for f in args.files.split(",") if f.strip()]:
        dtype = np.float32 if name.endswith(".f32") else np.int32
        a = load(os.path.join(args.a, name), dtype)
        b = load(os.path.join(args.b, name), dtype)
        if a is None or b is None:
            print(f"{name:>12}: MISSING ({'a' if a is None else 'b'})")
            ok = False
            continue
        same = a.size == b.size and np.array_equal(
            a.view(np.uint8), b.view(np.uint8))
        if same:
            print(f"{name:>12}: identical  {a.nbytes} bytes  {a.size} elements")
        else:
            ok = False
            print(f"{name:>12}: MISMATCH
